- **AUSTRIA → ITALY**: Last fall all three of us — A SER, F GRE, and F RUM — bounced each other out of BUL simultaneously, and nobody gained anything. This spring I'm proposing a clean solution: let A SER take BUL uncontested (I'll support with A BUD for strength 2), while your F GRE and F EAS press Turkey's home centers directly — AEG, CON, SMY. You gain more by cracking Turkey's home centers than by contesting BUL with me; I'll actively support your push toward CON or SMY if you stay out of BUL this turn.
- **AUSTRIA → RUSSIA**: Last fall F RUM → BUL/EC, F GRE → BUL/SC, and A SER → BUL all bounced each other — a wasted turn for all of us while France and Germany grew. This spring I'm asking you to hold F RUM and let A SER take BUL uncontested; in exchange, I'll keep A GAL firmly out of your northern position and support any Russian move against Turkey's F CON or ANK. If F RUM contests BUL again, we both lose and France wins.
- **AUSTRIA → TURKEY**: Turkey, your F CON is now adjacent to BUL — if you move it back to BUL before I can take it, we're back to conflict. I'm offering a clear deal: let A SER take BUL this spring unopposed, and I'll commit Austria's forces to holding that line rather than pressing toward CON or ANK. Italy's fleets in GRE and EAS are the real threat to your home centers; I'm not your primary problem.
- **ENGLAND → GERMANY**: Fall 1904 you committed to F NTH S F ENG - BEL — I'm holding you to that deal this spring. France is pushing south with WES and SPA/SC, so BEL may actually be vulnerable now; give me F NTH S F ENG - BEL and I'll move F ENG - BEL, and we finally crack France's northern position. I'm holding F NWY in place as agreed — confirm F NTH S F ENG - BEL.
- **ENGLAND → FRANCE**: You're at 6 centers and pushing further south with WES and SPA/SC — I respect the strategy, but BEL is exposed now that your fleets are committed westward. Tell me concretely: will you move A BEL away this spring so I can take it uncontested, and what do you want in return? Otherwise I'm pressing F ENG - BEL with German support this turn.
- **ENGLAND → RUSSIA**: France is at 6 centers and Germany at 6 — both are far ahead of the rest of us. I'm pressing hard against France in the west this spring with German support for BEL; if you can apply any pressure on Germany's eastern flank to keep them honest, that helps both of us. What are your intentions this turn?
- **FRANCE → ENGLAND**: Spring 1905: I'm proposing a clean deal — you order F NTH S A BEL - HOL, I order A BEL - HOL with A BUR support, and we take HOL at 3-strength guaranteed. Germany cannot hold. In return, I will support your move into DEN or KIE next fall with A HOL — that's your path to a 5th center and real leverage over Germany. This is the same deal I've offered before, but now my southern fleets are free to press Italy, so I don't need HOL as badly as you do; if you decline again, I redirect south and you stay stuck at 4.
- **FRANCE → GERMANY**: Spring 1905: I'm willing to finally stop pressing HOL if you give me something concrete in return.
